      }
      
      //change the displayed price
      var productSelector = '#product-' + productId;
      
      $(productSelector + ' > .product-item--price').each(function() {

      });

      var productId = '2174104043593';

      var avail = false;

          	avail = v.available;
        }

      }
      
      //change the displayed price
      var productSelector = '#product-' + productId;
      
      $(productSelector + ' > .product-item--price').each(function() {
        if ('price-' + productId + '-' + variantId == this.id) {
        	$(this).show();
        }
        else {
        	$(this).hide();
        }
        
       
      });     
    });

	var formSelector = '#addToCartForm-' + 1677658554441;
    var buySelector = '#addToCart-' + 1677658554441;
    var dropdownSelector = '#dropdownToggle-' + 1677658554441;
    
    var swatch = $(formSelector).find('input[name=id]');
    var buyButton = $(buySelector);
    var dropdownButton = $(dropdownSelector);
    
    buyButton.click(function(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      
      var productId = '1677658554441';      
      var variantId = $('#addToCartForm-' + '1677658554441').find('input[name=id]:checked').val();   
      $(this).find('.buy-text').hide();
      $(this).find('.btn-loader').show();
      $(this).prop('disabled', true);
      Shopify.addItemAndOpenCart(variantId, 1, '#addToCart-' + productId);
    });

    swatch.change(function(e) {     
      var productId = '1677658554441';
      var variantId = this.value;
      var variants = [{"id":15136648659017,"title":"3cc","option1":"3cc","option2":null,"option3":null,"sku":"8333_14_351","requires_shipping":true,"taxable":true,"featured_image":null,"available":true,"name":"DNM-37 - 3cc","public_title":"3cc","options":["3cc"],"price":499,"weight":14,"compare_at_price":499,"inventory_management":"shopify","barcode":null},{"id":15136648757321,"title":"10cc","option1":"10cc","option2":null,"option3":null,"sku":"8333_14_106","requires_shipping":true,"taxable":true,"featured_image":null,"available":true,"name":"DNM-37 - 10cc","public_title":"10cc","options":["10cc"],"price":999,"weight":14,"compare_at_price":999,"inventory_management":"shopify","barcode":null}]

      var avail = false;
      
      for (var i = 0; i < variants.length; i++) {
        var v = variants[i];
        if (v.id == variantId) {
          	avail = v.available;
        }
      }
      
      
      var linkSelector = '#link-' + productId;
      var productUrl = '/collections/featured/products/dnm-37';    
      
      if (avail) {
        $(linkSelector).attr("href", productUrl + "?variant=" + variantId);        
        $('#addToCart-' + productId).prop('disabled', false);
//         $('#addToCart-' + productId).text('BUY');
      }     
      else {
        $(linkSelector).attr("href", productUrl);   
        $('#addToCart-' + productId).prop('disabled', true);
//         $('#addToCart-' + productId).text('SOLD OUT');
      }
      
      //change the displayed price
      var productSelector = '#product-' + productId;
      
      $(productSelector + ' > .product-item--price').each(function() {
        if ('price-' + productId + '-' + variantId == this.id) {
        	$(this).show();
        }
        else {
        	$(this).hide();
        }
        
       
      });     
    });

      }

	var formSelector = '#addToCartForm-' + 4297195880521;
    var buySelector = '#addToCart-' + 4297195880521;
    var dropdownSelector = '#dropdownToggle-' + 4297195880521;
    
    var swatch = $(formSelector).find('input[name=id]');
    var buyButton = $(buySelector);
    var dropdownButton = $(dropdownSelector);
    
    buyButton.click(function(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      
      var productId = '4297195880521';      
      var variantId = $('#addToCartForm-' + '4297195880521').find('input[name=id]:checked').val();   
      $(this).find('.buy-text').hide();
      $(this).find('.btn-loader').show();
      $(this).prop('disabled', true);
      Shopify.addItemAndOpenCart(variantId, 1, '#addToCart-' + productId);
    });
    
//     dropdownButton.click(function(e) {
//       var productId = '4297195880521';
//       $('#dropdown-' + productId).show();
//     });
    
    
    swatch.change(function(e) {     
      var productId = '4297195880521';
      var variantId = this.value;
      var variants = [{"id":30940308602953,"title":"Phantom (Stickerless Bright)","option1":"Phantom (Stickerless Bright)","option2":null,"option3":null,"sku":"11432_1_332","requires_shipping":true,"taxable":true,"featured_image":{"id":13235990888521,"product_id":4297195880521,"position":1,"created_at":"2019-10-22T14:45:37-04:00","updated_at":"2019-10-22T14:45:37-04:00","alt":null,"width":900,"height":675,"src":"https:\/\/cdn.shopify.com\/s\/files\/1\/0035\/5205\/1273\/products\/twYpmirwTD6di.jpg?v=1571769937","variant_ids":[30940308602953]},"available":true,"name":"MFJS MeiLong Carbon Fiber Cubic Gift Box - Phantom (Stickerless Bright)","public_title":"Phantom (Stickerless Bright)","options":["Phantom (Stickerless Bright)"],"price":2399,"weight":447,"compare_at_price":null,"inventory_management":"shopify","barcode":null}]

      var avail = false;
      
      for (var i = 0; i < variants.length; i++) {
        var v = variants[i];
        if (v.id == variantId) {
          	avail = v.available;
        }
      }
      
      
      var linkSelector = '#link-' + productId;
      var productUrl = '/collections/new-arrivals/products/mfjs-meilong-carbon-fiber-cubic-gift-box';    
      
      if (avail) {
        $(linkSelector).attr("href", productUrl + "?variant=" + variantId);        
        $('#addToCart-' + productId).prop('disabled', false);
//         $('#addToCart-' + productId).text('BUY');
      }     
      else {
        $(linkSelector).attr("href", productUrl);   
        $('#addToCart-' + productId).prop('disabled', true);
//         $('#addToCart-' + productId).text('SOLD OUT');
      }
      
      //change the displayed price
      var productSelector = '#product-' + productId;
      
      $(productSelector + ' > .product-item--price').each(function() {
        if ('price-' + productId + '-' + variantId == this.id) {
        	$(this).show();
        }
        else {
        	$(this).hide();
        }
        
       
      });     
    });

          $('.swatch-mini-element.10cc').hide();

      }

	var formSelector = '#addToCartForm-' + 4296660484169;
    var buySelector = '#addToCart-' + 4296660484169;
    var dropdownSelector = '#dropdownToggle-' + 4296660484169;
    
    var swatch = $(formSelector).find('input[name=id]');
    var buyButton = $(buySelector);
    var dropdownButton = $(dropdownSelector);
    
    buyButton.click(function(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      
      var productId = '4296660484169';      
      var variantId = $('#addToCartForm-' + '4296660484169').find('input[name=id]:checked').val();   
      $(this).find('.buy-text').hide();
      $(this).find('.btn-loader').show();
      $(this).prop('disabled', true);
      Shopify.addItemAndOpenCart(variantId, 1, '#addToCart-' + productId);
    });
    
//     dropdownButton.click(function(e) {
//       var productId = '4296660484169';
//       $('#dropdown-' + productId).show();
//     });
    
    
    swatch.change(function(e) {     
      var productId = '4296660484169';
      var variantId = this.value;
      var variants = [{"id":30930333630537,"title":"Stickerless (Bright)","option1":"Stickerless (Bright)","option2":null,"option3":null,"sku":"11430_1_153","requires_shipping":true,"taxable":true,"featured_image":{"id":13229328597065,"product_id":4296660484169,"position":1,"created_at":"2019-10-21T13:51:38-04:00","updated_at":"2019-10-21T13:51:38-04:00","alt":null,"width":900,"height":675,"src":"https:\/\/cdn.shopify.com\/s\/files\/1\/0035\/5205\/1273\/products\/x9aLWeee016z9.jpg?v=1571680298","variant_ids":[30930333630537]},"available":true,"name":"QiYi DNA Pyraminx - Stickerless (Bright)","public_title":"Stickerless (Bright)","options":["Stickerless (Bright)"],"price":699,"weight":81,"compare_at_price":null,"inventory_management":"shopify","barcode":null}]

      var avail = false;
      
      for (var i = 0; i < variants.length; i++) {
        var v = variants[i];
        if (v.id == variantId) {
          	avail = v.available;
        }
      }
      
      
      var linkSelector = '#link-' + productId;
      var productUrl = '/collections/new-arrivals/products/qiyi-dna-pyraminx';    
      
      if (avail) {
        $(linkSelector).attr("href", productUrl + "?variant=" + variantId);        
        $('#addToCart-' + productId).prop('disabled', false);
//         $('#addToCart-' + productId).text('BUY');
      }     
      else {
        $(linkSelector).attr("href", productUrl);   
        $('#addToCart-' + productId).prop('disabled', true);
//         $('#addToCart-' + productId).text('SOLD OUT');
      }
      
      //change the displayed price
      var productSelector = '#product-' + productId;
      
      $(productSelector + ' > .product-item--price').each(function() {
        if ('price-' + productId + '-' + variantId == this.id) {
        	$(this).show();
        }
        else {
        	$(this).hide();
        }
        
       
      });     
    });
    
    $( document ).ready(function() {
      var collection = null;

      if (collection != null) {
        if (collection.title == '$60 Rewards') {
          $('#1828615290953-swatch-0-3cc').click();
          $('#1828615192649-swatch-0-3cc').click();

          $('.swatch-mini-element.10cc').hide();
      	}         
      }
   
    });

    var dropdownButton = $(dropdownSelector);
    
    buyButton.click(function(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      
      var productId = '4186067009609';      
      var variantId = $('#addToCartForm-' + '4186067009609').find('input[name=id]:checked').val();   
      $(this).find('.buy-text').hide();
      $(this).find('.btn-loader').show();

      var productId = '4186067009609';
      var variantId = this.value;
      var variants = [{"id":30552347836489,"title":"Stickerless (Bright)","option1":"Stickerless (Bright)","option2":null,"option3":null,"sku":"11423_1_153","requires_shipping":true,"taxable":true,"featured_image":{"id":12905252814921,"product_id":4186067009609,"position":1,"created_at":"2019-10-09T17:40:39-04:00","updated_at":"2019-10-09T17:40:39-04:00","alt":null,"width":900,"height":675,"src":"https:\/\/cdn.shopify.com\/s\/files\/1\/0035\/5205\/1273\/products\/iZnkmKITsHy2G.jpg?v=1570657239","variant_ids":[30552347836489]},"available":true,"name":"MFJS MeiLong Polaris Cube - Stickerless (Bright)","public_title":"Stickerless (Bright)","options":["Stickerless (Bright)"],"price":699,"weight":110,"compare_at_price":null,"inventory_management":"shopify","barcode":null}]

      var avail = false;
      
      for (var i = 0; i < variants.length; i++) {
        var v = variants[i];
        if (v.id == variantId) {
          	avail = v.available;
        }
      }
      
      
      var linkSelector = '#link-' + productId;
      var productUrl = '/collections/new-arrivals/products/mfjs-meilong-polaris-cube';

        $('#addToCart-' + productId).prop('disabled', false);

      else {

        $('#addToCart-' + productId).prop('disabled', true);

      }
      
      //change the displayed price
      var productSelector = '#product-' + productId;
      
      $(productSelector + ' > .product-item--price').each(function() {
        if ('price-' + productId + '-' + variantId == this.id) {
        	$(this).show();
        }
        else {
        	$(this).hide();
        }
        
       
      });     
    });

	var formSelector = '#addToCartForm-' + 4168701116489;
    var buySelector = '#addToCart-' + 4168701116489;
    var dropdownSelector = '#dropdownToggle-' + 4168701116489;
    
    var swatch = $(formSelector).find('input[name=id]');
    var buyButton = $(buySelector);
    var dropdownButton = $(dropdownSelector);
    
    buyButton.click(function(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      
      var productId = '4168701116489';      
      var variantId = $('#addToCartForm-' + '4168701116489').find('input[name=id]:checked').val();   
      $(this).find('.buy-text').hide();
      $(this).find('.btn-loader').show();
      $(this).prop('disabled', true);
      Shopify.addItemAndOpenCart(variantId, 1, '#addToCart-' + productId);
    });
    
//     dropdownButton.click(function(e) {
//       var productId = '4168701116489';
//       $('#dropdown-' + productId).show();
//     });
    
    
    swatch.change(function(e) {     
      var productId = '4168701116489';
      var variantId = this.value;
      var variants = [{"id":30304513884233,"title":"Stickerless (Bright)","option1":"Stickerless (Bright)","option2":null,"option3":null,"sku":"11420_1_153","requires_shipping":true,"taxable":true,"featured_image":{"id":12821514420297,"product_id":4168701116489,"position":1,"created_at":"2019-10-03T13:17:01-04:00","updated_at":"2019-10-03T13:17:01-04:00","alt":null,"width":900,"height":675,"src":"https:\/\/cdn.shopify.com\/s\/files\/1\/0035\/5205\/1273\/products\/LuatnQpxkEmXE.jpg?v=1570123021","variant_ids":[30304513884233]},"available":true,"name":"Pro Shop Mr. M Pyraminx - Stickerless (Bright)","public_title":"Stickerless (Bright)","options":["Stickerless (Bright)"],"price":2399,"weight":103,"compare_at_price":null,"inventory_management":"shopify","barcode":null}]

      var avail = false;
      
      for (var i = 0; i < variants.length; i++) {
        var v = variants[i];
        if (v.id == variantId) {
          	avail = v.available;
        }
      }
      
      
      var linkSelector = '#link-' + productId;
      var productUrl = '/collections/new-arrivals/products/cubicle-mr-m-pyraminx';    
      
      if (avail) {
        $(linkSelector).attr("href", productUrl + "?variant=" + variantId);        
        $('#addToCart-' + productId).prop('disabled', false);
//         $('#addToCart-' + productId).text('BUY');
      }     
      else {
        $(linkSelector).attr("href", productUrl);   
        $('#addToCart-' + productId).prop('disabled', true);
//         $('#addToCart-' + productId).text('SOLD OUT');
      }
      
      //change the displayed price
      var productSelector = '#product-' + productId;
      
      $(productSelector + ' > .product-item--price').each(function() {
        if ('price-' + productId + '-' + variantId == this.id) {
        	$(this).show();
        }
        else {
        	$(this).hide();
        }
        
       
      });     
    });
    
    $( document ).ready(function() {
      var collection = null;

      if (collection != null) {
        if (collection.title == '$60 Rewards') {
          $('#1828615290953-swatch-0-3cc').click();
          $('#1828615192649-swatch-0-3cc').click();

          $('.swatch-mini-element.10cc').hide();
      	}         
      }
   
    });

  </script>

	var formSelector = '#addToCartForm-' + 4168612446281;
    var buySelector = '#addToCart-' + 4168612446281;
    var dropdownSelector = '#dropdownToggle-' + 4168612446281;
    
    var swatch = $(formSelector).find('input[name=id]');
    var buyButton = $(buySelector);
    var dropdownButton = $(dropdownSelector);
    
    buyButton.click(function(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      
      var productId = '4168612446281';      
      var variantId = $('#addToCartForm-' + '4168612446281').find('input[name=id]:checked').val();   
      $(this).find('.buy-text').hide();
      $(this).find('.btn-loader').show();
      $(this).prop('disabled', true);
      Shopify.addItemAndOpenCart(variantId, 1, '#addToCart-' + productId);
    });
    
//     dropdownButton.click(function(e) {
//       var productId = '4168612446281';
//       $('#dropdown-' + productId).show();
//     });
    
    
    swatch.change(function(e) {     
      var productId = '4168612446281';
      var variantId = this.value;
      var variants = [{"id":30304329695305,"title":"Stickerless (Bright)","option1":"Stickerless (Bright)","option2":null,"option3":null,"sku":"11418_1_153","requires_shipping":true,"taxable":true,"featured_image":{"id":12821414543433,"product_id":4168612446281,"position":1,"created_at":"2019-10-03T12:03:25-04:00","updated_at":"2019-10-03T12:03:25-04:00","alt":null,"width":900,"height":675,"src":"https:\/\/cdn.shopify.com\/s\/files\/1\/0035\/5205\/1273\/products\/GJgrf3QqzWhWt.jpg?v=1570118605","variant_ids":[30304329695305]},"available":true,"name":"FangShi Mini 6x6 - Stickerless (Bright)","public_title":"Stickerless (Bright)","options":["Stickerless (Bright)"],"price":2299,"weight":133,"compare_at_price":null,"inventory_management":"shopify","barcode":null}]

      var avail = false;
      
      for (var i = 0; i < variants.length; i++) {
        var v = variants[i];
        if (v.id == variantId) {
          	avail = v.available;
        }
      }
      
      
      var linkSelector = '#link-' + productId;
      var productUrl = '/collections/new-arrivals/products/fangshi-mini-6x6';    
      
      if (avail) {
        $(linkSelector).attr("href", productUrl + "?variant=" + variantId);        
        $('#addToCart-' + productId).prop('disabled', false);
//         $('#addToCart-' + productId).text('BUY');
      }     
      else {
        $(linkSelector).attr("href", productUrl);   
        $('#addToCart-' + productId).prop('disabled', true);
//         $('#addToCart-' + productId).text('SOLD OUT');
      }
      
      //change the displayed price
      var productSelector = '#product-' + productId;
      
      $(productSelector + ' > .product-item--price').each(function() {
        if ('price-' + productId + '-' + variantId == this.id) {
        	$(this).show();
        }
        else {
        	$(this).hide();
        }
        
       
      });     
    });
    
    $( document ).ready(function() {
      var collection = null;

      if (collection != null) {
        if (collection.title == '$60 Rewards') {
          $('#1828615290953-swatch-0-3cc').click();
          $('#1828615192649-swatch-0-3cc').click();

          $('.swatch-mini-element.10cc').hide();
      	}         
      }
   
    });

<script>
$(document).ready( function() {
	$('a[href^="http"]').not('a[href^="'+location.protocol+'//'+location.hostname+'"]').not('a[href^="/"]').attr('target', '_blank');
});
  $('.search-bar').submit(function(e) {
  	e.preventDefault();
    
    var form = $(this);
    
    var query = form.find('input[id=query]').val();
    var type = form.find('select').val().replace(/ /g, "-");
    var terms = type == 'ALL' ? query : 'product_type:' + type + ' ' + query;

    form.append('<input type="hidden" name="q" value="' + terms + '">');
    
    var formData = form.serializeArray();
    
    this.submit();
  });
  
</script>
